Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on parabolic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 593. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 75 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_593(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 593. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^75(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any parabolic module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 624. The projective resolution has length at most 217 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by parabolic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 624, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 217. QED.
